Law of Conservation of Charge ^m8xPLxXe

The total net charge inside a closed system remains constant over time
Charge can never be created or destroyed, only transferred

Quantization of Charge ^pwYfZvoB

Net charge must be an integral multiple of e
    ex. Q = 4e or 8e or 143e
but never fractional numbers (1.5, 0.75, etc.)
Charge is discrete, not continuous ^I8NCn2Hb

Follow rule 3, write the equation for the
net force of each axis (x and y)
    - There is no other vector in the x axis
      aside from F    so you will add 0 in the equation
            ex. F    = F     + 0
    - Since F    also lies in the y axis and is facing opposite
      to F    we can subtract the two to get the net y force
      of B

Region surrounding an object where electric force is felt
The objects do not directly interact with each other

    - to get the Magnitude of the Electric Field check the power of the
